<h1>Update 214199 — Balance & Fixes</h1>
<p class="plage">Patch notes from 213106 to 214199</p>
<h2 class="banniere"><img src="https://clan.akamai.steamstatic.com/images/41657262/cccf3f97161987ff7301b1a7bddbf10a96661886.png" alt="Patch highlights"></h2>
<h3>NATO</h3>
<ul>
<li><b>PILATUS PC-9</b> — Price 80 → 45, Weapon − M2HB x2, Hydra 70</li>
<li><b>CH-54B TARHE</b> — Price 45 → 40</li>
<li><b>HU.21</b> — Price 45 → 40</li>
<li><b>SEK</b> — Price 40 → 45, Weapon + Flashbang</li>
<li><b>SUPER PUMA</b> — Price 45 → 40</li>
<li><b>M48A5</b> — Price 90 → 95</li>
</ul>
<h3>PACT</h3>
<ul>
<li><b>KTJ SZÖCSKE (AA)</b> — Optics Mediocre → Very Good</li>
<li><b>T-62 Obr. 75 INSTRUKT.</b> — Price 110 → 90</li>
<li><b>TANK TIP-62</b> — Price 50 → 60, Speed 45 km/h → 60 km/h</li>
<li><b>BPD</b> — Price 45 → 55</li>
<li><b>FUSILEROS MANDO</b> — Price 45 → 55, Trait − IFV</li>
<li><b>OBUZ TIP-56 122mm</b> — Price 100 → 90, Weapon D-30 → M-30</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<h2 class="banniere"><img src="https://clan.akamai.steamstatic.com/images/41657262/d129ff742bec57e0e10ee6e5922cc51fb3161bd9.png" alt="Fixes"></h2>
<h3>Gameplay</h3>
<ul>
<li>Fixed the interface staying hidden when a game ended while the UI was hidden with the Hide In-Game UI shortcut, which prevented leaving the game normally</li>
<li>Fixed the <b>BTR-60-30-2</b> (Cuba) having no visible crew: its driver and 30mm gunner are now displayed</li>
<li>Fixed the <b>WOMBAT</b> 120mm recoilless rifle HE weapon using the <b>M40A1</b> weapon icon, it now has its own icon</li>
<li>Fixed the <b>AMX-13 LAR-160 [CLU]</b> (Venezuela) having the same HE damage, splash and suppression as the HE version</li>
<li>Fixed the <b>AAP-MP-100</b> (Cuba) gunner using a US soldier model instead of a Cuban one</li>
<li>Reduced the chance of AA guns starting fires in forests</li>
</ul>
<h3>Army General</h3>
<ul>
<li>Cuba: fixed the static PACT AA units of the "Capture San Cristobal" secondary objective only being able to intercept US aircraft once, they can now intercept every turn</li>
<li>Cuba: fixed the 3-Y OTD. MSB, 4-Y OTD. MSB and 20-Y OTD. MSB battalions being marked as auxiliary battalions instead of combat battalions</li>
</ul>
<hr>

## Upstream NDF content changes

Diffs show changes from the previous game baseline to the current one. Mod-affected files appear first, followed by selected large gameplay files. Standard excerpts are limited to 200 lines and 20,000 characters per file, and 500,000 characters total; standard files over 512,000 bytes or 5,000 lines are omitted. The six selected gameplay files have separate limits of 64 MiB per source, 30 seconds per comparison, and 2,000 lines or 200,000 bytes per excerpt.
